Title: Mirabilia Dei inter Indicos, or the Rise and Progress of a remarkable Work of Grace amongst a number of the Indians in the provinces of New-Jersey and Pennsylvania, justly represented in a Journal kept by order of the honourable Society in Scotland for Propagating Christian Knowledge
Creator: David Brainerd (1718-1747)
Publisher: Philadelphia: printed and sold by William Bradford
Publication Date: 1746
Rights: Public Domain (published 1746, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item mirabiliadeiinte00brai (https://archive.org/details/mirabiliadeiinte00brai), the item's own mirabiliadeiinte00brai_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Duke University Libraries; host metadata date: 1746. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 5bbc82f6ac04fc2441ff2eb17cb0a8001ee6ace2c02293b6e60f07459a0ce39b.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Duke University scan; readable with the long s rendered as f; library marks precede the title page and are left in place.
Content note: Brainerd's own public Journal of the Crossweeksung and Cranbury revival among the Delaware, June 1745 to June 1746, as he sent it to the Scottish Society; the Native awakening in the missionary's own report, with the Society's attestations.
